def _prepare_stable_whatsapp_profile():
    """
    Perfil rápido:
    - se já existe, retorna imediatamente;
    - só procura versões antigas quando o perfil está vazio;
    - sem rglob/tamanho recursivo durante o startup.
    """
    try:
        ALIYVO_USER_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        marker=ALIYVO_USER_DIR/"profile_ready.flag"

        if marker.exists():
            PROFILE_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            return

        try:
            if PROFILE_DIR.exists() and any(PROFILE_DIR.iterdir()):
                marker.write_text("ok",encoding="utf-8")
                return

            release=getattr(win,"_release_whatsapp_focus",None)
            if callable(release):release()
            try:win.activateWindow()
            except Exception:pass
        except Exception:pass
        if target is None:return
        try:target.setFocus(Qt.FocusReason.OtherFocusReason)
        except Exception:pass

        for delay in (0,60,160):
            try:
                QTimer.singleShot(delay,lambda t=target:t.setFocus(Qt.FocusReason.OtherFocusReason) if t is not None and t.isVisible() else None)
            except Exception:pass

    def eventFilter(self,obj,event):
        try:
            if obj in (getattr(self,"valor",None),getattr(self,"msg",None)):
                if event.type() in (QEvent.Type.MouseButtonPress,QEvent.Type.FocusIn):
                    # Libera o WebView2 imediatamente; o clique continua normalmente.
                    try:
                        win=self.window();web=getattr(win,"web",None)
                        if web is not None:setattr(web,"_aliyvo_focus_blocked",True)
                        release=getattr(win,"_release_whatsapp_focus",None)
                        if callable(release):release()
                    except Exception:pass
                    QTimer.singleShot(0,lambda t=obj:self._claim_editor_focus(t))
        except Exception:pass
        return super().eventFilter(obj,event)

    def parse_valor(self):
        s=self.valor.text().strip().replace("R$","").replace(" ","")
        if not s:
            return None
        if "," in s:
            s=s.replace(".","").replace(",",".")
        try:
            return float(s)
        except:
            return None

    def runJavaScript(self, script, callback=None):
        return self.owner._run_javascript_compat(script,callback)


class WhatsAppView(QtWebView2Widget):
    """
    WhatsApp via Microsoft Edge WebView2.

    Vídeos, chamadas e codecs são responsabilidade do Edge, como no teste
    aprovado. O restante do ALIYVO continua usando a API compatível
    page().runJavaScript para WA-JS e automações existentes.
    """
    loadFinished=pyqtSignal(bool)
    loadStarted=pyqtSignal()
    _download_done=pyqtSignal(str,bool,bool)

---

    def profile(self):
        return self._profile

    def runJavaScript(self, script, callback=None):
        return self.owner._run_javascript_compat(script,callback)

    def _configure_webview2_core(self,core):
        self._core_webview2=core

        # Downloads do próprio WhatsApp.
        try:
            core.DownloadStarting += self._on_webview2_download_starting
        except Exception:
            pass

        # microfone/câmera/notificações quando a origem for WhatsApp.

            core.PermissionRequested += self._on_webview2_permission_requested
        except Exception:
---
            pass

            core.PermissionRequested += self._on_webview2_permission_requested
        except Exception:

        # Mantém autoplay/mídia sob responsabilidade do Edge.
        try:
            settings=core.Settings
            settings.IsScriptEnabled=True
        except Exception:
            pass

    def _run_javascript_compat(self,script,callback=None):
        """
        QWebEngine.runJavaScript accepts an expression and returns its raw result.
        QtWebView2.evaluate_js executes a function body and returns
        {'success':..., 'result':...}. This adapter translates both semantics.
        """
        code=str(script or "")
        stripped=code.strip()

        # IIFEs / Boolean(...) / simple expressions need an explicit return
        # inside qtwebview2's async wrapper.
        expression_prefixes=(
            "(",
            "Boolean(",
            "!!",
            "typeof ",
---
            "window.",
            "document.",
            "[",
        )

        if callback is not None and stripped.startswith(expression_prefixes):
            expr=stripped
            while expr.endswith(";"):
                expr=expr[:-1].rstrip()
            code="return ("+expr+");"

        def finished(payload):
            if callback is None:
                return
            try:
                if isinstance(payload,dict) and payload.get("success"):
                    callback(payload.get("result"))
                else:
                    callback(None)
            except Exception:
                try:
---
            while expr.endswith(";"):
                expr=expr[:-1].rstrip()
            code="return ("+expr+");"

        def finished(payload):
            if callback is None:
                return
            try:
                if isinstance(payload,dict) and payload.get("success"):
                    callback(payload.get("result"))
                else:
                    callback(None)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    callback(None)
                except Exception:
                    pass

        try:
            if callback is None:
                self.evaluate_js(code)
---

            except Exception:
                try:
                    callback(None)
                except Exception:

        try:

                self.evaluate_js(code)
            else:

    def _extract_visible_whatsapp_chats(self, callback):
        js = r"""
        (() => {
          try {
            const pane =
              document.querySelector("#pane-side") ||
              document.querySelector("[aria-label*='Chat list' i]") ||
              document.querySelector("[aria-label*='Lista de conversas' i]");

            if (!pane) return {ok:false,error:"Lista de conversas não encontrada"};

            const rows = Array.from(
              pane.querySelectorAll("[role='listitem'], [role='row'], div[tabindex='-1']")
            );

            const chats=[];
---
          } catch(e){
            return {ok:false,error:String(e)};
          }
        })();
        """
        self.window().web.page().runJavaScript(js, callback)

    def choose_whatsapp_contacts(self):
        self._extract_visible_whatsapp_chats(self._show_whatsapp_contact_picker)

    def _show_whatsapp_contact_picker(self, result):

            )
            return

        if not chats:

    def _initialized(self,*args):
        self._ready=True
        self.status.setText("Catálogo carregado")
        if self._pending_search:
            plate,callback,result_callback=self._pending_search
            self._pending_search=None
            QTimer.singleShot(1200,lambda:self.search_plate(plate,callback,result_callback))

    def closeEvent(self,event):
        # Depois do login, continua ativo em segundo plano para buscas diretas.
        event.ignore()
        self.hide_background()

    def evaluate(self,code,callback=None):
        try:
            self.web.evaluate_js(code,callback)
        except Exception as e:
            if callback:
                callback({"success":False,"error":str(e)})

    def search_plate(self,plate,callback=None,result_callback=None):
        # Busca normal inteiramente em segundo plano. Antes de cada consulta,
        # garante que o catálogo esteja na tela de busca por placa.
        self.hide_background()
        plate=re.sub(r"[^A-Z0-9]","",str(plate or "").upper())[:7]
        if len(plate)!=7:
            if callback: callback({"ok":False,"error":"Placa inválida."})
            return

        if not self._ready:
            self._pending_search=(plate,callback,result_callback)
            self.hide_background()
            self.status.setText("Inicializando fonte técnica em segundo plano...")
            return

        self._last_search_token+=1
---

            self.hide_background()
            self.status.setText("Inicializando fonte técnica em segundo plano...")
            return

        self._last_search_token+=1
        token=self._last_search_token
        self._search_attempt(plate,callback,result_callback,token,0)

    def read_network_capture(self,callback):
        js=r'''return (() => {
          try{ const PREFIX="ALIYVO_CAPTURE::"; let arr=[]; try{ if((window.name||"").startsWith(PREFIX)) arr=JSON.parse(window.name.slice(PREFIX.length)||"[]"); }catch(e){} return {ok:true,events:arr,currentUrl:location.href||"",title:document.title||"",text:(document.body&&document.body.innerText||"").slice(0,8000)}; }
          catch(e){return {ok:false,error:String(e)};}
        })();'''
        def done(payload): callback(self._unwrap_js(payload))
        self.evaluate(js,done)

    def _diagnostic_finish_live_call(self,now=None,reason="overlay_gone"):
        import time
        now=float(now or time.time());cur=getattr(self,"_diagnostic_live_call",None)
        if not isinstance(cur,dict):self._diagnostic_live_call=None;self._diagnostic_live_missing=0;return
        client=str(cur.get("client") or "").strip() or "Contato";start=float(cur.get("start_ts") or now)
        duration=max(max(0,int(cur.get("duration_seconds") or 0)),max(0,int(now-start)))
        session=str(cur.get("session_id") or f"live|{client}|{int(start)}")
        self._diagnostic_log("call",client=client,direction=str(cur.get("direction") or "unknown"),call_type=str(cur.get("call_type") or "voice"),missed=False,duration_seconds=duration,call_key=session,source="live_overlay",started_ts=start,ended_ts=now,end_reason=reason)
        recent=list(getattr(self,"_diagnostic_recent_live_calls",[]) or []);recent.append({"client":client,"ended_ts":now,"duration_seconds":duration,"call_type":str(cur.get("call_type") or "voice")})
        self._diagnostic_recent_live_calls=[x for x in recent[-12:] if now-float(x.get("ended_ts") or now)<900]
        self._diagnostic_live_call=None;self._diagnostic_live_missing=0

        now=float(now or time.time())

    def _ai_compact_event(self,x):
        if not isinstance(x,dict):return None
        keep={}
        for k in ("time","event","client","text","response_seconds","total_wait_seconds","until_open_seconds","after_open_seconds","tool","direction","call_type","duration_seconds","interruptions","max_unread_during_wait","unread_count","content_type","needs_reply","snippet"):
            if k in x and x.get(k) not in (None,""):
                v=x.get(k)
                if isinstance(v,str):v=v[:420]
                keep[k]=v
        return keep or None

    def _handle_whatsapp_media_download(self, path):
        try:
            ext=Path(path).suffix.lower()
            audio_exts={".ogg",".opus",".mp3",".m4a",".wav",".webm",".aac"}

            if ext not in audio_exts:
                return

            # Só abre/transcreve automaticamente se o painel de áudio estiver aberto.
            # Assim downloads normais não interrompem o usuário.
            if not self.audio_panel.isVisible():
                return

            self._transcribe_downloaded_audio(path)
        except:
            pass

def _aliyvo_runtime_exception_hook(exc_type,exc_value,exc_tb):
    try:
        folder=ALIYVO_USER_DIR/"logs"
        folder.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        text="".join(
            traceback.format_exception(exc_type,exc_value,exc_tb)
        )
        (folder/"ALIYVO_RUNTIME_ERRO.txt").write_text(
            text,
            encoding="utf-8"

    def eventFilter(self,obj,event):
        try:
            if obj in (getattr(self,"valor",None),getattr(self,"msg",None)):
                if event.type() in (QEvent.Type.MouseButtonPress,QEvent.Type.FocusIn):
                    # Libera o WebView2 imediatamente; o clique continua normalmente.
                    try:
                        win=self.window();web=getattr(win,"web",None)
                        if web is not None:setattr(web,"_aliyvo_focus_blocked",True)
                        release=getattr(win,"_release_whatsapp_focus",None)
                        if callable(release):release()
                    except Exception:pass
                    QTimer.singleShot(0,lambda t=obj:self._claim_editor_focus(t))
        except Exception:pass
        return super().eventFilter(obj,event)

    def mousePressEvent(self,event):

    def parse_valor(self):
        s=self.valor.text().strip().replace("R$","").replace(" ","")
        if not s:
            return None
        if "," in s:
            s=s.replace(".","").replace(",",".")
        try:
            return float(s)
        except:
            return None

    def _run_javascript_compat(self,script,callback=None):
        """
        QWebEngine.runJavaScript accepts an expression and returns its raw result.
        QtWebView2.evaluate_js executes a function body and returns
        {'success':..., 'result':...}. This adapter translates both semantics.
        """
        code=str(script or "")
        stripped=code.strip()

        # IIFEs / Boolean(...) / simple expressions need an explicit return
        # inside qtwebview2's async wrapper.
        expression_prefixes=(
            "(",
            "Boolean(",
            "!!",
            "typeof ",
---
            "window.",
            "document.",
            "[",
        )

        if callback is not None and stripped.startswith(expression_prefixes):
            expr=stripped
            while expr.endswith(";"):
                expr=expr[:-1].rstrip()
            code="return ("+expr+");"

        def finished(payload):
            if callback is None:
                return
            try:
                if isinstance(payload,dict) and payload.get("success"):
                    callback(payload.get("result"))
                else:
                    callback(None)
            except Exception:
                try:
---
            while expr.endswith(";"):
                expr=expr[:-1].rstrip()
            code="return ("+expr+");"

                if isinstance(payload,dict) and payload.get("success"):
                    callback(payload.get("result"))
                else:
                    callback(None)
            except Exception:
                try:
                    callback(None)
                except Exception:
                    pass

        try:
            if callback is None:
                self.evaluate_js(code)

          } catch(e){
            return {ok:false,error:String(e)};
          }
        })();

    def search_plate(self,plate,callback=None,result_callback=None):
        # Busca normal inteiramente em segundo plano. Antes de cada consulta,
        # garante que o catálogo esteja na tela de busca por placa.
        self.hide_background()
        plate=re.sub(r"[^A-Z0-9]","",str(plate or "").upper())[:7]
        if len(plate)!=7:
            if callback: callback({"ok":False,"error":"Placa inválida."})
            return

        if not self._ready:
            self._pending_search=(plate,callback,result_callback)
            self.hide_background()
            self.status.setText("Inicializando fonte técnica em segundo plano...")
            return

        self._last_search_token+=1

            return

            self._pending_search=(plate,callback,result_callback)
            self.hide_background()
            self.status.setText("Inicializando fonte técnica em segundo plano...")
            return

        self._last_search_token+=1
        token=self._last_search_token
        self._search_attempt(plate,callback,result_callback,token,0)

    def start_network_capture(self,callback=None,reset=True):
        self._capture_started=True
        reset_js='true' if reset else 'false'
        js=r"""return (() => {
          try{
            const PREFIX="ALIYVO_CAPTURE::";
            const doReset=__RESET__;
            const redact=(v)=>{
              let s=String(v==null?"":v);
              s=s.replace(/((?:password|senha|token|csrf|authorization|session|sessao)[^=&:\\s]{0,30}[=:]\\s*)[^&\\s,}]+/gi,"$1***");
              return s.slice(0,6000);
            };
            const read=()=>{ try{ if((window.name||"").startsWith(PREFIX)) return JSON.parse(window.name.slice(PREFIX.length)||"[]"); }catch(e){} return []; };
            const save=(arr)=>{ try{ window.name=PREFIX+JSON.stringify(arr.slice(-220)); }catch(e){} };
            const log=(kind,obj)=>{ const arr=read(); arr.push(Object.assign({time:new Date().toISOString(),kind:kind,url:location.href},obj||{})); save(arr); };
            if(doReset) save([]);
---
            return {ok:true,message:"Captura ativa nesta página",href:location.href};
          }catch(e){ return {ok:false,error:String(e)}; }
        })();""".replace('__RESET__',reset_js)
        def done(payload):
            result=self._unwrap_js(payload)
            if callback: callback(result)
        self.evaluate(js,done)

          catch(e){return {ok:false,error:String(e)};}
        })();'''
        def done(payload): callback(self._unwrap_js(payload))
        self.evaluate(js,done)

    def _diagnostic_finish_live_call(self,now=None,reason="overlay_gone"):
        import time
        now=float(now or time.time());cur=getattr(self,"_diagnostic_live_call",None)
        if not isinstance(cur,dict):self._diagnostic_live_call=None;self._diagnostic_live_missing=0;return
        client=str(cur.get("client") or "").strip() or "Contato";start=float(cur.get("start_ts") or now)
        duration=max(max(0,int(cur.get("duration_seconds") or 0)),max(0,int(now-start)))
        session=str(cur.get("session_id") or f"live|{client}|{int(start)}")
        self._diagnostic_log("call",client=client,direction=str(cur.get("direction") or "unknown"),call_type=str(cur.get("call_type") or "voice"),missed=False,duration_seconds=duration,call_key=session,source="live_overlay",started_ts=start,ended_ts=now,end_reason=reason)
        recent=list(getattr(self,"_diagnostic_recent_live_calls",[]) or []);recent.append({"client":client,"ended_ts":now,"duration_seconds":duration,"call_type":str(cur.get("call_type") or "voice")})
        self._diagnostic_recent_live_calls=[x for x in recent[-12:] if now-float(x.get("ended_ts") or now)<900]
        self._diagnostic_live_call=None;self._diagnostic_live_missing=0

        def block(label,data):
            resp=[int(x.get("total_wait_seconds") or x.get("response_seconds") or 0) for x in data if x.get("event")=="response"]
            opens=sum(1 for x in data if x.get("event")=="chat_opened")
            cust=sum(1 for x in data if x.get("event")=="message_customer")
            seller=sum(1 for x in data if x.get("event")=="message_seller")
            calls=[x for x in data if x.get("event")=="call"]
            callsec=sum(max(0,int(x.get("duration_seconds") or 0)) for x in calls)

    def _ai_compact_event(self,x):
        if not isinstance(x,dict):return None
        keep={}
        for k in ("time","event","client","text","response_seconds","total_wait_seconds","until_open_seconds","after_open_seconds","tool","direction","call_type","duration_seconds","interruptions","max_unread_during_wait","unread_count","content_type","needs_reply","snippet"):
            if k in x and x.get(k) not in (None,""):
                v=x.get(k)
                if isinstance(v,str):v=v[:420]
                keep[k]=v
        return keep or None

    def _ai_learning_path(self):
        return USER_DATA_DIR/"ia_observadora_aprendizado.json"

    def _ai_learning_empty(self):
        return {"version":2,"last_processed_ts":0.0,"patterns":{},"seller_phrases":{},"seen_cases":{},"seen_phrase_cases":{},"updated_at":""}

    def _ai_profiles_cache_refresh_sync(self,force=False):
        import time
        cache=self._ai_profiles_cache_load()
        age=time.time()-float(cache.get("built_at_ts") or 0)
        if not force and cache.get("profiles") and age<300:return cache.get("profiles") or {}
        profiles=self._ai_client_profiles_rebuild_fast()
        self._ai_profiles_cache_save(profiles)
        return profiles

    def _ai_profiles_cache_refresh_async(self,force=False):
        import threading,time
        if bool(getattr(self,"_ai_profiles_refresh_busy",False)):return
---
        return profiles

    def _ai_client_profile_payload(self,client_name):
        name=str(client_name or "").strip()
        profiles=self._ai_client_profiles_payload() or {}
        return profiles.get(name) or {"client":name,"patterns":[],"outcomes":[],"confidence":"observando","interaction_cases":0,"calls":0,"audio":0,"wait_over_5m":0,"interruptions":0}

    def _handle_whatsapp_media_download(self, path):
        try:
            ext=Path(path).suffix.lower()
            audio_exts={".ogg",".opus",".mp3",".m4a",".wav",".webm",".aac"}

            if ext not in audio_exts:
                return

            # Só abre/transcreve automaticamente se o painel de áudio estiver aberto.
            # Assim downloads normais não interrompem o usuário.
            if not self.audio_panel.isVisible():
                return

            self._transcribe_downloaded_audio(path)
        except:
            pass
